We evaluate EDMD-kDL against state-of-the-art ANN-based approaches on a range of numerical experiments, including global sea-surface-temperature forecasting and learning directly from video data. Across all tested settings, EDMD-kDL achieves performance comparable to or better than the ANN-based methods. Moreover, in contrast to standard kernel methods, the proposed approach is scalable to large datasets by design since the size of the required kernel matrices depends on the number of collocation points rather than the size of the training dataset.

</details>


### [174] [Marformer: A Transformer for Predicting Missing Data Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12379)
*Prabhav Singh,Xiheng Tom Wang,Haojun Shi,Jason Eisner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Marformer，一种直接预测条件边缘分布的Transformer。它借鉴BERT的掩码预测范式，为每个变量构建隐向量表示并通过注意力迭代细化。在贝叶斯网络、离散多元高斯和结构化标注数据上，Marformer匹配或超越经典缺失数据方法，且速度显著更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常在不完全信息下进行。当只能观测部分随机变量时，需预测缺失变量。缺失变量上的条件边缘分布是计算贝叶斯风险和信息价值（VOI）的关键。现有方法往往需要完整联合分布建模或依赖数据生成过程的领域知识，效率与通用性受限。

Method: 提出Marformer：一个被训练来在给定任意观测值集合时直接预测条件边缘分布的Transformer。类似BERT预测缺失词，Marformer为每个分布p(X_i)构建隐向量表示，并通过注意力机制向其他分布p(X_j)迭代细化。该方法不建模完整联合分布，不需要数据生成过程的领域知识，且所有预测在单次前向传播中完成。

Result: 在三个含缺失数据的合成域（贝叶斯网络、离散多元高斯、结构化标注数据）上评估，即使经典缺失数据方法被给予生成数据的真实模型族和先验，Marformer仍能匹配或超越这些方法。在真实标注数据集上，Marformer在最大训练规模下超越所评估的基线方法。两类情况下，Marformer均显著快于所评估的生成式基线。

Conclusion: Marformer无需完整联合建模和领域知识，单次前向即可直接预测条件边缘分布，在合成与真实数据上匹配或超越经典方法，同时速度显著更快，为缺失数据下的贝叶斯风险与VOI计算提供了高效通用方案。

Abstract: Real decisions are made under incomplete information. If we observe only some of the random variables we need, we can predict the others. The \textbf{conditional marginals} over the missing variables are the key ingredient for computing Bayes risk and Value of Information (VOI), the expected gain from acquiring one more observation before deciding. We present the Marformer, a Transformer trained to directly predict conditional marginals given any set of observed values. Like BERT, which is trained to predict missing words from context, the Marformer constructs a hidden-vector representation for each distribution $p(X_i)$ and iteratively refines it through attention to other distributions $p(X_j)$. Unlike generative approaches, the Marformer does not model the full joint distribution, requires no domain knowledge of the data-generating process, and makes all predictions in a single forward pass. We evaluate across three synthetic domains with missing data---Bayesian networks, discretized multivariate Gaussians, and structured annotation data. The Marformer can match or outperform classical missing-data methods, even when those methods are given the true model family and prior that generated the synthetic data. We also evaluate on a real annotation dataset, where the Marformer outperforms the evaluated baselines at the largest training size. In both cases, the Marformer is substantially faster than the evaluated generative baselines.

</details>


### [175] [Long Text to Predictive Features: LLM-Guided Blockwise Feature Engineering via Executable Program Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12390)
*Ziming Dai,Dabiao Ma,Ziheng Guo,Jack Dong,Zimu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLM-BlockFE，一种 LLM 引导的离线特征构建框架，将长文本转换为可执行特征程序，从而在在线推理时无需调用 LLM。该方法通过增量追加不可变代码块构建特征程序，并引入基于深度校准信用分配的块级回滚机制与多轨迹交错搜索，以缓解贪心搜索次优问题。在四个数据集上相较最强基线实现 AUC 绝对提升 0.0069–0.0358，并在五个已部署金融风控应用中相较人工策略实现 KS 绝对提升 0.02–1.56 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业风控系统通常依赖结构化数据模型进行高效预测，但大量有价值信息蕴含于非结构化长文本中。人工特征工程成本高，而在线推理阶段逐条调用 LLM 又难以满足实际部署要求（延迟、成本等）。因此需要一种既能挖掘长文本信息、又能在线上避免 LLM 调用的方案。

Method: LLM-BlockFE 将长文本转换为可执行的特征程序，并冻结后部署用于在线结构化特征抽取。其核心包括：（1）通过增量追加不可变代码块（block）构建特征程序，并利用下游模型评估候选特征；（2）针对传统贪心搜索易陷入次优解的问题，引入块级回滚机制，基于深度校准的信用分配实现回滚；（3）交错推进多条独立搜索轨迹，并通过共享各轨迹探索方向的固定描述来减少冗余探索。

Result: 在两个公开数据集和两个私有数据集的全量对比中，LLM-BlockFE 相较各数据集上的最强基线取得 AUC 绝对提升 0.0069–0.0358。在五个已部署的金融风控应用中，上线后监控显示相较现有人工设计策略取得 KS 绝对提升 0.02–1.56 个百分点。

Conclusion: LLM-BlockFE 通过离线构建可执行特征程序，在避免在线 LLM 调用的同时有效利用长文本信息，并在公开/私有数据集及实际金融风控部署中均验证了其相较基线与人工策略的稳定增益，兼具效果与工程可用性。

Abstract: Industrial risk-control systems typically rely on structured-data models for efficient prediction, yet substantial valuable information remains embedded in unstructured long text. Extracting this information through manual feature engineering is labor-intensive, while requiring a large language model (LLM) to process every real-time input may not meet practical deployment requirements. To address this challenge, we propose LLM-BlockFE, an LLM-guided offline feature construction framework that converts long text into executable feature programs, thereby avoiding LLM calls during online inference. LLM-BlockFE constructs feature programs by incrementally appending immutable code blocks and evaluates candidate features using a downstream model. To address the tendency of conventional greedy search to become trapped in suboptimal solutions, our method introduces a block-level rollback mechanism based on depth-calibrated credit allocation and advances multiple independent search trajectories in an interleaved manner, reducing redundant exploration by sharing fixed descriptions of each trajectory's exploration direction. After the search, the resulting programs are frozen and deployed to extract structured features for downstream prediction models. Across two public and two private datasets, LLM-BlockFE achieves absolute AUC improvements of 0.0069 to 0.0358 over the strongest baseline on each dataset in the full-dataset comparison. Post-launch monitoring across five deployed financial risk-control applications shows absolute KS improvements of 0.02 to 1.56 percentage points over the existing manually designed strategy.

</details>


### [176] [Prospective Prediction of OOD Degradation from Source-Side Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12397)
*Sasha,Monin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究能否仅用源端训练动力学在OOD退化被直接观测之前预测其发生。在受控的捷径学习设定中，基于逻辑回归的预测器展现出明确的前瞻信号，而仅用训练时间则不能；源端量的时间摘要显著优于其当前值，且置信度与熵动力学在CNN到MLP的零样本迁移中仍保持预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布外（OOD）性能退化通常只有在部署或直接评估时才被发现，缺乏事前预警手段。作者希望验证一个更早期、更低成本的可行性问题：能否只依赖源端训练过程中的动力学信号，在OOD退化真正出现之前进行预测。

Method: 采用受控的捷径学习（shortcut-learning）设定制造可预期的OOD退化；从源端训练动力学提取特征，构建简单的逻辑回归预测器，并与仅使用训练时间（training time）的基线对比；同时比较源端量的“时间摘要”（temporal summaries）与其“当前值”的信息量；进一步做跨模型迁移实验，将CNN上得到的结论不加额外训练地迁移到MLP，检验置信度与熵动力学的预测能力。

Result: 逻辑回归预测器在受控设定中产生了清晰的、具有前瞻性的预警信号，而训练时间本身不具预测力；源端量的时间摘要在信息量上明显优于其当前值；从CNN迁移到MLP（无额外训练）时，置信度与熵的训练动力学仍保留可观的预测信息。

Conclusion: 结果构成一个概念验证（proof of principle）：源端训练动力学中可能蕴含着面向未来OOD失败的早期预警信号，为训练期监测与事前风险预测提供了可行方向，但其普适性与更广泛场景下的有效性仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: We study whether persistent out-of-distribution (OOD) degradation can be predicted before it is directly observed using only source-side training dynamics. In a controlled shortcut-learning setting, a simple logistic regression predictor develops a clear prospective signal, while training time alone does not. Temporal summaries of the source-side quantities are substantially more informative than their current values. When transferred without additional training from a CNN to an MLP, confidence and entropy dynamics retain substantial predictive information. These results provide a proof of principle that source-side training dynamics can contain an early warning signal for future OOD failure.

</details>


### [177] [Learning Kilometer-Scale Weather Prediction with Global-Regional Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12401)
*Guowen Li,Yang Liu,Yujie Wang,Qiuyan Sun,Haoyuan Liang,Juepeng Zheng,Hong Cheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ScaleCast区域天气预报框架，通过全局-区域对齐复用预训练全球模型指导公里级高分辨率预报，无需针对不同全球驱动重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公里级区域天气预报对本地预警和天气敏感决策至关重要。现有数据驱动方法依赖数值预报提供大尺度引导，或需额外训练全球预报组件，成本高且复用性差；预训练全球天气模型是高效的大尺度预报来源，但其与区域预测的耦合面临跨网格表征对齐、全局引导与局地相互作用融合等难题。

Method: 提出ScaleCast框架，核心为Global-Regional Alignment机制：Global-Regional Conversion模块将联合的全局与区域表征对齐到区域位置；Global-Regional Alignment and Dynamics模块将对齐后的引导与区域邻域相互作用结合，推进区域状态演化。

Result: 基于ERA5（0.25°）与CERRA（5.5 km）的实验中，地面及高空变量的区域预报均有提升；单一训练模型可支持Pangu-Weather、GraphCast、HRES等多种全球预报驱动而无需专门重训。在HRRR（3 km）上微调验证了框架对不同区域域和空间分辨率的适应性。风暴案例显示气旋定位与核心气压估计改善，与HadISD站点观测的温度、湿度局地变化更吻合。

Conclusion: ScaleCast通过全局-区域对齐有效复用预训练全球模型，实现可迁移、多驱动兼容的公里级区域预报，并具备跨域跨分辨率适应能力。

Abstract: Kilometer-scale regional weather forecasting is essential for local weather warnings and weather-sensitive decisions. Existing data-driven approaches often rely on numerical forecasts for large-scale guidance or require additional training of global forecasting components. Pretrained global weather models offer an efficient source of large-scale forecasts, motivating their reuse to guide high-resolution regional prediction. However, this coupling requires aligning global and regional representations across different grids and integrating global guidance with local interactions to advance regional states. We propose ScaleCast, a regional forecasting framework that addresses these challenges through Global-Regional Alignment. Its Global-Regional Conversion module aligns joint global and regional representations with regional locations, while the Global-Regional Alignment and Dynamics block combines aligned guidance with regional neighborhood interactions. Experiments using ERA5 global analyses on a 0.25-degree grid and CERRA regional reanalysis at 5.5 km spacing demonstrate improved regional forecasts across surface and upper-air variables, with a single trained model supporting multiple global forecast drivers (i.e., Pangu-Weather, GraphCast, and HRES) without specific retraining. Fine-tuning on HRRR at 3 km spacing further demonstrates the framework's adaptability to a different regional domain and spatial resolution. Windstorm case studies show improved cyclone positioning and core-pressure estimates, while comparisons with HadISD station observations show closer agreement with local temperature and humidity changes.

</details>


### [178] [A Unified Bellman Operator for Safety-Critical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12420)
*Nishanth Arun Rao,Royina Karegoudra Jayanth,Benjamin Eysenbach,Jaime Fernández Fisac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种新的贝尔曼算子，将性能与安全目标统一为联合值函数，并证明其时间差分学习在双时间尺度随机逼近下收敛，收敛后策略在最大化任务回报的同时始终满足安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键领域要求兼顾任务性能与严格安全约束；现有安全强化学习方法存在两难：要么依赖先验知识（如安全滤波器）以提供严格安全保证，要么支持联合学习但仅能在平均意义上满足安全约束。

Method: 提出将性能与安全目标统一到联合值函数的新型贝尔曼算子，并用双时间尺度随机逼近框架分析其时间差分学习：快时间尺度估计学习联合策略的安全值，慢时间尺度估计联合值；通过将极限动力学形式化为占用平均微分包含并证明其渐近收敛到一组极限最优安全约束任务值函数。

Result: 理论上，收敛后的最优策略在最大化任务回报的同时始终保持安全；在连续控制任务上结合神经网络逼近的实验表明收敛稳定，测试时安全违规接近零。

Conclusion: 该工作为同时实现高性能与严格安全约束的安全强化学习提供了统一的理论框架与收敛保证，并在连续控制任务中验证了其有效性与近零安全违规表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in safety-critical domains requires maximizing task performance while strictly adhering to safety constraints. Existing safe reinforcement learning paradigms typically force a trade-off: they either require a priori knowledge to provide strict safety guarantees (e.g., safety filters), or they enable joint learning but only satisfy safety constraints on average. In this work, we propose a novel Bellman operator that unifies performance and safety objectives into a joint value function. We show that temporal difference learning with the joint Bellman operator converges under a two-timescale stochastic approximation framework. On the fast timescale, the safety value of the learning joint policy is estimated, while the joint value is estimated on the slow timescale. Convergence is ensured by formulating the limiting dynamics as an occupation-averaged differential inclusion, and showing that it asymptotically converges to a set of limiting optimal safety-constrained task value functions. Theoretically, once converged, the resulting optimal policy maximizes task return while maintaining safety at all times. Empirical evaluations on continuous control tasks with neural approximations demonstrate stable convergence with near-zero safety violations at test time.

</details>


### [179] [Rounding in Preconditioner Space: Redesigning 4-bit AdamW Optimizer-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12444)
*Hanyang Li,Shao Tang,Daniel Thomas Braithwaite,Gregory Dexter,Leonardo Neves,Aman Gupta,Hiroto Udagawa,Abhishek Shivanna,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"舍入空间"视角重新设计AdamW的4-bit优化器状态量化，提出ZIP-SR与ZE-EDEN两种方案，在130M–2.7B参数预训练中均缩小与32-bit AdamW的验证损失差距，最大降幅达70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化AdamW优化器状态可降低持久化存储开销，但量化误差会沿动量递推传播并扰动后续自适应更新，因此需要重新审视量化所处的坐标系以抑制误差影响。

Method: 以"舍入空间"（量化器在相邻重建电平间选择的坐标）为核心：对二阶动量分析零附近量化单元，指出状态空间小误差不保证下一步预条件子空间小误差；并用一维二次函数构造说明两空间舍入导致定性不同的优化动力学。据此前提出ZIP-SR（二阶码本保留零、在预条件子空间计算随机舍入概率）与ZE-EDEN（二阶码本排除零，并重缩放量化块以缓解正量化地板引起的预条件子失真）。两者一阶动量均用4-bit NF4，并在训练最后10%对LM-head一阶动量做定向随机舍入。

Result: 在GPT与Llama风格、130M至2.7B参数的预训练实验中，两种方法在每个评测规模下都缩小了TorchAO 4-bit AdamW相对32-bit AdamW的平均验证损失差距，最大降幅达70%；全参数监督微调中验证损失低于TorchAO且下游任务接近32-bit AdamW。

Conclusion: 舍入空间的选择（状态空间 vs 预条件子空间）是4-bit优化器状态量化的关键设计维度，ZIP-SR与ZE-EDEN通过针对性的空间选择与码本设计有效抑制误差传播，在预训练与微调中兼顾存储效率与优化质量。

Abstract: Quantizing AdamW's optimizer states reduces persistent storage, but quantization errors propagate through the moment recurrences and perturb subsequent adaptive updates. We redesign 4-bit optimizer-state quantization for AdamW from the perspective of \emph{rounding space}: the coordinate in which a quantizer chooses between adjacent reconstruction levels. For the second moment, a local analysis of the quantization cell adjacent to zero shows that small mean state error need not imply small mean preconditioner error at the next step. A one-dimensional quadratic construction further shows qualitatively different optimization dynamics under state-space and preconditioner-space rounding. These results motivate Zero-Inclusive Preconditioner-space Stochastic Rounding (\textbf{ZIP-SR}), which retains zero in the second-moment codebook and computes stochastic-rounding probabilities in preconditioner space. As a complementary route, Zero-Excluding EDEN calibration (\textbf{ZE-EDEN}) uses a zero-excluding second-moment codebook and rescales the quantized second-moment block to mitigate the preconditioner distortion caused by the positive quantization floor. Both configurations use 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) for the first moment, with targeted stochastic rounding of the LM-head first moment during the final 10\% of training. Across GPT- and Llama-style pretraining experiments ranging from \textbf{130M} to \textbf{2.7B} parameters, both methods reduce TorchAO 4-bit AdamW's mean validation-loss gap to 32-bit AdamW at every evaluated model size, with the largest reported gap reduction reaching \textbf{70\%}. In full-parameter supervised fine-tuning, both recipes achieve lower validation loss than TorchAO while remaining close to 32-bit AdamW on downstream tasks.

</details>


### [180] [Caught in the Act: Probes Effectively Detect Sabotage and Catch Unverbalized Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12445)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Alex F. Spies,Tigist Diriba,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出可扩展的白盒欺骗检测探针方法，通过构建迄今最大的欺骗数据集FIBS并设计跨层跨token聚合的新型探针架构，在前沿监控场景中实现了高精度欺骗检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期事件凸显了对LLM智能体进行监控的困难以及模型欺骗人类的风险，亟需可扩展到前沿监控场景的欺骗检测手段。

Method: 收集迄今最大的欺骗数据集用于训练探针；提出一种新型探针架构，能够跨多个层和多个token聚合信息；并将探针应用于仅凭上下文无法判断欺骗的情形（称为内省式欺骗）。

Result: 在SHADE-Arena上达到98.8% AUC，超过Opus 5.5文本监控基线，且随底层模型规模增大效果提升；在内省式欺骗评估中，探针区分含模型真实隐藏目标的文本与含其他目标的文本，AUC最高达99.7%；探针还能检测主流开放权重模型在政治敏感话题上以及受压力时关于自身信念的欺骗。

Conclusion: 白盒探针可有效扩展到前沿监控场景，并发布训练数据集FIBS，以推动有效探针在前沿部署中的应用，同时鼓励社区补充更多欺骗与破坏行为样本。

Abstract: Recent incidents have highlighted the challenge of monitoring LLM agents and the danger of models deceiving people. We show that white-box deception detection via probes can be scaled up to frontier monitoring settings by collecting the largest deception dataset to date for training probes and introducing a novel probe architecture which can aggregate information across many layers and tokens. Our probes achieve 98.8% AUC in SHADE-Arena, surpassing an Opus 5.5 text-monitoring baseline, and show improved efficacy as the underlying model is scaled up. To push our probes to their limit, we test them on several cases where deception cannot be determined from the context alone. In these cases, which we refer to as introspective deception, the ground truth can only be determined through careful elicitation or thorough knowledge of a model's training data. In one such evaluation, we show that probes can distinguish transcripts containing a model's true hidden goal from other goals with an AUC of up to 99.7%. Our probes also readily detect deception on prominent open-weight models which lie about politically sensitive topics, and about their beliefs when put under pressure. We release our training dataset, dubbed FIBS, to help drive frontier deployment of effective probes, and encourage the community to expand upon it with further examples of deception and sabotage.

</details>


### [181] [Bi-FORK: Generative Modeling of High-Dimensional Bifurcating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12449)
*Anna Zimmel,Fleur Hendriks,Markus Holzleitner,Florian Sestak,Martin Weichselbaumer,Vlado Menkovski,Johannes Brandstetter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bi-FORK，一个基于潜空间流匹配与排斥引导采样的生成式框架，用于学习高维物理系统中分岔导致的"一对多"解映射，并在多种分岔场景中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分岔在结构屈曲、流体与气候动力学等物理系统中普遍存在，但在深度学习领域仍未被充分探索。在对称破缺分岔处，同一输入对应多个同样有效的解，这违背了多数已学习物理代理模型所依赖的一对一假设。现有方法难以刻画这种多模态解结构，且在高维系统上的扩展性不足。

Method: 提出Bi-FORK生成式框架：通过潜空间流匹配（latent flow matching）生成完整轨迹，以保持空间与时间一致性；并采用排斥引导采样（repulsion-guided sampling），在单次摊销式（amortized）前向过程中恢复不同的解分支。

Result: 在屈曲梁、机械超材料以及Allen-Cahn相分离三个任务上进行评估，覆盖连续、离散与场值分岔，离散化规模最高达260,000个点。Bi-FORK成功恢复了多模态解结构，且相较此前方法在规模上提升了数个数量级。

Conclusion: Bi-FORK将生成式建模拓展至高维分岔物理系统，为学习一对多物理代理模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Bifurcations are ubiquitous in physical systems, from structural buckling to fluid and climate dynamics, yet they remain largely unexplored in deep learning. At a symmetry-breaking bifurcation, a single input admits multiple equally valid solutions, violating the one-to-one assumption underlying most learned physical surrogates. We introduce Bi-FORK, a generative framework for learning these one-to-many solution maps in high-dimensional systems. Bi-FORK generates complete trajectories through latent flow matching, preserving space and time coherence, and uses repulsion-guided sampling to recover distinct solution branches in a single amortized pass. We evaluate Bi-FORK on buckling beams, mechanical metamaterials, and Allen-Cahn phase separation, spanning continuous, discrete, and field-valued bifurcations with discretizations up to 260,000 points. Bi-FORK recovers the multimodal solution structure while scaling several orders of magnitude beyond prior approaches, opening generative modeling to high-dimensional bifurcating physical systems.

</details>


<div id='bioRxiv.Neuroscience'></div>

# bioRxiv.Neuroscience [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Hippocampal extracellular matrix remodeling and temporally distinct glial reorganization following peripheral injury](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.30.755817v1)
*Ge, C.,Kausar, V.,Xu, L.,Maghsoudi, A.,Shtudland, G.,Amrami, M.,Betancourt, M.,Niaz, A.,Zirkiyev, J.,Alvarado, S. G.,Abzalimov, R.,He, Y.,Tajerian, M.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 外周损伤后海马细胞外基质(ECM)与胶质细胞表型呈时间依赖性演变，基质生物力学可能是微胶质功能的调节因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性疼痛常伴焦虑等情感障碍，但外周损伤如何通过分子与细胞变化导致脑功能持久改变尚不清楚；海马ECM调控突触可塑性、细胞信号与神经免疫互作，可能是疼痛相关中枢可塑性的候选机制。海马ECM、胶质形态与情感行为在损伤后的时序变化研究不足，亟需系统刻画。

Method: 采用胫骨骨折/石膏固定模型；在损伤后3、7、20周进行行为学测试（机械超敏与两种焦虑相关范式）；在1、3、7、20周对微胶质与星形胶质进行高维形态计量分析；慢性期用去细胞化结合MALDI-TOF质谱成像定位海马ECM相关分子信号；体外用BV2微胶质考察刚度依赖的形态、吞噬与钙信号变化。

Result: 损伤小鼠在早期与慢性期出现机械超敏及两种焦虑相关范式中探索减少，20周时行为表型不再检出。7周时海马ECM相关肽信号改变，包括4种ECM相关蛋白信号降低。微胶质与星形胶质密度及多维形态呈不同时间依赖性变化，部分改变在行为指标回归对照后仍持续。体外BV2微胶质表现出刚度依赖的形态、吞噬和钙信号变化。

Conclusion: 外周损伤后海马ECM与胶质细胞表型呈时序性演变，且部分胶质改变独立于行为恢复；基质生物力学被识别为微胶质功能的候选调控因素，为疼痛相关中枢可塑性提供ECM—胶质互作机制线索。

Abstract: Chronic pain is frequently accompanied by anxiety and other affective disturbances, but the molecular and cellular changes linking peripheral injury to persistent alterations in brain function remain incompletely defined. The hippocampal extracellular matrix (ECM) regulates synaptic plasticity, cellular signaling, and neuroimmune interactions and may therefore contribute to pain-related central plasticity. We used a tibial fracture/cast immobilization model to examine temporal changes in hippocampal ECM, glial morphology, and affective behavior following peripheral injury. Behavioral testing was performed at 3, 7, and 20 weeks after injury, while high-dimensional morphometric analysis of microglia and astrocytes was performed at 1, 3, 7, and 20 weeks. Hippocampal ECM associated molecular signals were mapped at the chronic stage using decellularization coupled with MALDI-TOF mass spectrometry imaging. Injured mice developed mechanical hypersensitivity and reduced exploration in two anxiety related paradigms during the early and chronic stages, with these behavioral phenotypes no longer detected at 20 weeks. At 7 weeks, injury was associated with changes in hippocampal ECM-associated peptide signals, including reduced signals corresponding to four ECM-associated proteins. Microglia and astrocytes exhibited distinct, time-dependent changes in density and multidimensional morphology, with some alterations persisting after behavioral measures returned toward control levels. In vitro, BV2 microglia exhibited rigidity-dependent changes in morphology, phagocytic behavior, and calcium signaling. Together, these findings define a temporally evolving hippocampal ECM and glial phenotype following peripheral injury and identify matrix biomechanics as a candidate regulator of microglial function.

</details>


### [183] [Dorsomedial striatal Bmal1 deletion in medium spiny neurons alters EPSCs and dendritic morphology after chronic exposure to alcohol in female mice.](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.03.753483v1)
*Darvishmolla, M.,Courtemanche, R.,Amir, S.,Chapman, A.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究通过全细胞膜片钳记录发现，敲除生物钟基因Bmal1会阻断雌鼠背内侧纹状体D1型中等棘状神经元中酒精诱导的sEPSC振幅增强，同时揭示D2型神经元中类似的增强效应，从而解释Bmal1缺失降低饮酒行为的突触机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纹状体生物钟基因可调节雌鼠饮酒行为，但其突触机制尚不清楚。纹状体中等棘状神经元（MSNs）表达D1或D2型多巴胺受体，且二者受酒精的差异化调控，因此有必要在电生理鉴定的D1、D2神经元中探究生物钟基因Bmal1缺失对兴奋性突触传递的影响。

Method: 采用全细胞膜片钳记录，在电生理鉴定的雌鼠背内侧纹状体（DMS）D1与D2神经元中检测自发兴奋性突触后电流（sEPSCs），比较野生型与Bmal1敲除小鼠在有无间歇性酒精暴露条件下的差异，并结合biocytin填充观察D1 MSNs的树突分支与复杂度。

Result: 在野生型小鼠的推定D1神经元中，既往间歇性酒精暴露增加了sEPSC振幅而不影响频率，并伴随biocytin填充的D1 MSNs树突分支与复杂度增加。Bmal1敲除在D1神经元中产生相反效应，在酒精暴露与未暴露动物中均降低sEPSC振幅。在D2神经元中，酒精在野生型小鼠仅引起sEPSC振幅增加的非显著趋势，而在Bmal1敲除小鼠中则引起显著增加。

Conclusion: DMS局部Bmal1缺失可阻断酒精诱导的D1神经元sEPSC增强，同时揭示D2神经元中类似的增强效应。这些发现有助于解释DMS中Bmal1缺失如何降低雌鼠的酒精摄入。

Abstract: Circadian clock genes in the striatum modulate alcohol drinking behavior in female mice, but the underlying synaptic mechanisms remain unknown. Medium spiny neurons (MSNs) of the striatum express D1- or D2-type dopamine receptors, which are differentially modulated by alcohol. Here we used whole-cell patch-clamp recordings to examine how deletion of the clock gene Bmal1 affects spontaneous excitatory postsynaptic currents (sEPSCs) in electrophysiologically identified D1 and D2 neurons of the female mouse dorsomedial striatum (DMS). Prior intermittent access to alcohol increased sEPSC amplitude in putative D1 neurons of wildtype mice without affecting sEPSC frequency, and this increase was accompanied by greater dendritic branching and complexity in biocytin-filled D1 MSNs. Bmal1 knockout had the opposite effect in D1 neurons, reducing sEPSC amplitude in both alcohol-exposed and alcohol-naive animals. In D2 neurons, alcohol produced only a non-significant trend toward increased sEPSC amplitude in wildtype mice, but a significant increase in Bmal1 knockout mice. Thus, local disruption of Bmal1 appears to block the alcohol-induced enhancement of sEPSCs in D1 neurons while unmasking a similar enhancement in D2 neurons. These findings may help explain how Bmal1 deletion in the DMS reduces alcohol consumption in female mice.

</details>


### [184] [Rewiring the Human Brain: On the Fabric of Associative Thinking](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.04.17.718886v2)
*Czappa, F.,Kaster, M.,Kaiser, M.,Chen, X.,Butz-Ostendorf, M.,Wolf, F.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该论文通过计算模拟，提出概念细胞的结构性记忆印痕（engram）形成模型：依靠结构可塑性而非仅依赖已存在的突触，解释远距离神经元如何建立新的突触连接，并展示概念激活可通过重叠的感觉关联触发相关概念（感知—概念回路）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念细胞由异质、多模态感觉输入激活，其学习过程需将感知皮层细胞与概念细胞联结成联想记忆印痕。传统Hebbian模型依赖已有突触的强化，但脑内低边缘密度使这些细胞间存在直接突触的可能性很低，导致此类机制效率低、易遗忘。因此需要探讨更持久的、基于结构可塑性（de novo突触生成）的印痕形成机制，但其难点在于神经元如何在远距离找到新的突触后靶点。

Method: 在健康被试的连接组（connectomes）中模拟结构性印痕的形成，提出一种在结构层面形成印痕的模型假设，解释远距离神经元如何建立新突触连接。基于该模型进一步模拟概念激活过程，说明其如何通过重叠的感觉关联触发相关概念。

Result: 模型能够模拟出结构性概念印痕的形成过程，并提供神经元远距离寻找新突触后靶点的可能机制；同时展示概念激活可经由重叠的感觉关联引发相关概念的激活，形成所谓"感知—概念回路"（percept-concept loop）。

Conclusion: 持久的概念印痕可能依赖结构可塑性（新突触生成）而非仅靠已有突触的Hebbian强化；该结构层面的模型可解释概念印痕的形成及概念间的关联激活，为理解联想记忆与概念表征提供新框架。

Abstract: Concept cells are neurons in the medial temporal lobe that represent an abstract concept, such as a familiar person, in a context-independent way. They are activated by heterogeneous and potentially multimodal sensory input related to the concept, such as viewing a photo of the person, reading the person's name, or hearing the person's voice. Learning the concept implies connecting the cortical cells that are triggered when such features are perceived with the cells of the concept, resulting in an associative memory engram. Traditional models explain the formation of such an engram with Hebbian learning through synaptic plasticity, the strengthening of existing synapses in response to co-activation. However, the low edge density of the brain suggests that direct connections in the form of preexisting synapses between these cells are relatively unlikely, rendering such constructs inefficient and volatile. Instead, it is plausible that more persistent concept engrams rely on structural plasticity, involving the creation of synapses de novo. Yet, it remains unclear how neurons can find new postsynaptic targets over such a considerable distance. In this paper, we simulate the formation of such structural engrams in the connectomes of healthy subjects, offering a model hypothesis of how such engrams can be formed on a structural level. Based on our model, we further demonstrate how activating a concept can trigger related concepts through overlapping sensory associations, in a process we call a percept-concept loop.

</details>


### [185] [Disease-associated variants alter a novel physiological role of hnRNP H2 in neuronal development](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.02.756225v1)
*Lourinho, A.,Silva, M. S.,Tahiri, E.,D'Ambrosio, M.,Pinto, A. D.,Calvo Parra, S.,De Pietro, G.,Galvao, G.,Caniceiro, A. B.,Pereira de Almeida, L.,Pinheiro, P.,Moreira, I. S.,Nobre, R. J.,Mele, M.,Cwetsch, A. W.,Duarte, C. B.,Costa, R. O.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究发现hnRNP H2在海马神经元中表达并定位于突触区，其敲低会导致神经元形态、树突棘成熟、突触传递及皮层迁移缺陷；疾病相关变异体改变蛋白定位与活动依赖性突触募集，从而引发神经发育障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hnRNP H2的NLS和qRRM结构域突变与严重的HNRNPH2相关神经发育障碍有关，但hnRNP H2在神经元发育和突触成熟中的生理作用，以及疾病变异体如何改变这些过程导致病理，仍不清楚。

Method: 在体外培养的海马神经元及体内皮层中，检测hnRNP H2的表达与亚细胞定位；通过敲低（knockdown）评估其功能缺失效应；并引入疾病相关变异体，分析其对蛋白定位、活动依赖性突触募集以及神经元表型的影响。

Result: hnRNP H2表达于海马神经元并定位于突触区。敲低后神经元形态、树突棘成熟、突触传递及体内皮层神经元迁移均出现缺陷。疾病相关变异体改变蛋白定位和活动依赖性突触募集，导致神经元形态、体内皮层迁移与层状定向、树突棘成熟及突触功能异常。

Conclusion: hnRNP H2是神经发育的关键调控因子，其功能缺失及疾病相关变异体通过影响定位与突触募集导致神经发育异常，为HNRNPH2相关神经发育障碍提供了分子基础。

Abstract: Heterogeneous nuclear ribonucleoprotein H2 (hnRNP H2) is a member of the hnRNP family of RNA-binding proteins that regulate neuronal function through multiple aspects of RNA metabolism. Mutations in the nuclear localisation signal (NLS) and quasi-RNA recognition motifs (qRRMs) of hnRNP H2 have recently been linked to a severe developmental syndrome, termed HNRNPH2-related neurodevelopmental disorder. Despite its emerging importance, the physiological roles of hnRNP H2 in neuronal development and synaptic maturation, and how disease-associated variants alter these processes to drive pathology, remain poorly understood. Here, we show that hnRNP H2 is expressed in hippocampal neurons and localises to the synaptic compartment. Knockdown of hnRNP H2 revealed essential roles of the protein in the regulation of neuronal morphology, dendritic spine maturation, synaptic transmission, and cortical neuronal migration in vivo. Disease-associated hnRNP H2 variants altered protein localisation and activity-dependent synaptic recruitment, resulting in defects in neuronal morphology, in vivo cortical neuronal migration and laminar orientation, dendritic spine maturation, and synaptic function. Together, these findings establish hnRNP H2 as a critical regulator of neurodevelopment and identify a molecular basis for HNRNPH2-related neurodevelopmental disorder.

</details>


### [186] [Molecular basis of chemosensing by internal sensory neurons in Drosophila larvae](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2024.01.20.576342v2)
*Lu, S.,Qian, C. S.,Khair-Eldin, A.,Grueber, W. B.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 果蝇幼虫气管树突(td)神经元可感知O2下降与CO2升高，其低氧反应依赖Gyc88E/Gyc89Db，CO2反应依赖Gr28b.c，并揭示内感受化学感觉的分子机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内感受神经元监测机体化学与物理状态，对维持稳态和生存至关重要，但其环路与分子基础尚不清楚。果蝇幼虫气管树突(td)神经元沿呼吸器官延伸树突，为研究内部化学感觉提供了可操作的模型，因此作者旨在解析其感知呼吸气体的分子机制。

Method: 利用果蝇幼虫td神经元，结合钙成像等功能检测手段，鉴定对O2下降和CO2升高有反应的部分重叠神经元群体；通过遗传学手段检测非典型可溶性鸟苷酸环化酶(Gycs)和味觉受体(Grs)的作用；并在突变体中靶向表达Gr28b.c同源异构体进行功能回补与异位敏感性测试；同时观察幼虫挖掘行为中的神经元激活情况。

Result: 发现部分重叠的td神经元群体分别响应O2下降和CO2升高。Gyc88E/Gyc89Db为低氧诱发活动所必需，而Gr28b为CO2反应所必需。在td神经元中靶向表达Gr28b.c异构体可恢复突变体幼虫的CO2反应，并在td网络中赋予异位CO2敏感性。此外，气体敏感的td神经元在幼虫持续挖掘（一种类自然的觅食相关行为）时被激活。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了内感受神经元监测呼吸气体的分子机制，确立了Gyc88E/Gyc89Db与Gr28b.c分别在低氧和CO2感知中的关键作用，并推进了果蝇作为研究内部化学感觉神经与分子基础的模型。

Abstract: Interoceptive neurons monitor the chemical and physical state of the body, providing critical information to the central nervous system that maintains homeostasis and promotes survival. The circuit and molecular basis for these interoceptive senses remain under active investigation. A population of larval Drosophila sensory neurons, the tracheal dendrite (td) neurons, extend dendrites along respiratory organs and provide a tractable model for investigating the cellular and molecular basis of internal chemosensation. Here, we show that partially overlapping populations of td neurons respond to decreases in O2 and increases in CO2. To define the molecular basis of these responses, we examined the roles of atypical soluble guanylyl cyclases (Gycs) and gustatory receptors (Grs). We found that Gyc88E/Gyc89Db were required for hypoxia-evoked activity, whereas Gr28b was required for responses to CO2. Targeted expression of Gr28b.c isoform in td neurons restored responses to CO2 in mutant larvae and conferred ectopic sensitivity to CO2 within the td network. We further found that gas-sensitive td neurons were activated upon prolonged larval burrowing, a natural-like feeding-associated behavior. Together, these findings reveal molecular mechanisms that enable interoceptive neurons to monitor respiratory gases and advance a Drosophila model for studying the neural and molecular basis of internal chemosensation.

</details>


### [187] [PTEN Subcellular Localization Dictates Function](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.06.16.732743v2)
*Desmet, N. M.,Griffin, C. F.,Seo, H.,OuYang, A.,Tir, P.,Prina, M. L.,Wang, W.,Li, M.,Luikart, B. W.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究通过对发育中海马神经元进行HaloTag活细胞成像及逆转录病毒介导的Pten敲除与定位基序融合回补实验，证明PTEN的亚细胞定位是其调控神经元形态的关键决定因素，不同定位的PTEN对胞体、树突和树突棘的调控作用存在空间分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTEN突变会导致PI3K通路异常激活，引起神经元肥大并与自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）密切相关，且多种PTEN突变会改变其亚细胞定位；然而定位如何决定PTEN在发育神经元中的功能尚不清楚，因此需要直接检验亚细胞靶向对神经元形态的调控作用。

Method: 1）利用HaloTag标记的PTEN进行活细胞成像，观察其在神经元中的动态定位；2）采用逆转录病毒介导的遗传操作，在发育中海马神经元中敲除内源性Pten，同时表达融合不同定位基序的PTEN；3）通过核排除PTEN、经srGAP3的FBAR结构域靶向丝状伪足、经Homer1C靶向突触后致密区以及核定位PTEN等不同定位变体，直接评估亚细胞靶向对神经元形态的影响。

Result: Pten缺失导致胞体增大、树突分支更复杂、树突棘密度、长度和头部面积增加。核排除PTEN可完全挽救上述表型；靶向丝状伪足（FBAR）或突触后致密区（Homer1C）的PTEN可纠正全部形态异常，且相比野生型使树突分支简化。相反，核定位PTEN仅部分挽救，可正常化胞体大小和树突棘头部面积，但不能恢复树突复杂度和树突棘密度。

Conclusion: PTEN通过局部作用限制神经元的生长和结构连接，而树突棘头部大小的调控可由核内和核外PTEN共同介导，可能涉及转录或剪接依赖机制。该研究确定亚细胞定位是PTEN功能的关键决定因素，并揭示了PTEN塑造神经元发育的空间差异性机制。

Abstract: Mutations in phosphatase and tensin homolog (PTEN) drive unregulated activation of the phosphatidylinositol-3-kinase (PI3K) pathway, resulting in neuronal hypertrophy, and are strongly associated with autism spectrum disorder (ASD). Several PTEN mutations alter subcellular localization, yet how localization governs PTEN function in developing neurons remains unclear. Although PTEN has been reported broadly distributed throughout neurons, here, live imaging of HaloTagged PTEN reveals dynamically regulated localization, suggesting spatial control of its signaling. We then used retroviral-mediated genetic manipulation to delete endogenous Pten in developing hippocampal neurons while simultaneously expressing PTEN fused to defined localization motifs, allowing us to directly test how subcellular targeting regulates neuronal morphology. Loss of Pten produces neurons characterized by enlarged somata, more elaborate dendritic arbors, and increased spine density, length, and head area. Nuclear-excluded PTEN fully rescued these phenotypes, whereas targeting PTEN to filopodia via fusion to the FBAR domain of srGAP3 or to the postsynaptic density via Homer1C corrected or corrected all morphological abnormalities in PTEN-deficient neurons and simplified dendritic arborization compared to wild-type. In contrast, nuclear-localized PTEN produced only partial rescue, normalizing soma size and spine head area but not dendritic complexity or spine density. These findings indicate that PTEN acts locally to restrain growth and structural connectivity, whereas regulation of spine head size can be mediated by PTEN both inside and outside the nucleus, potentially through transcriptional or splicing-dependent mechanisms. Together, our results identify subcellular localization as a critical determinant of PTEN function and reveal spatially distinct mechanisms through which PTEN sculpts neuronal development.

</details>


### [188] [MICROSTRUCTURAL ORGANIZATION OF CORTICO-SUBCORTICAL FUNCTIONAL CONVERGENCE](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.02.756182v1)
*Verma, P.,Singh, S.,Dang, S.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究利用MICA-MICs多模态MRI数据集（50名健康成人），刻画了与皮层下靶点功能汇聚的皮层区域的微结构组织，揭示了皮层-皮层下功能组织的互补维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类皮层下结构通过功能组织化的皮层-皮层下系统整合来自分布式皮层区域的信息，但功能汇聚皮层区域的微结构组织特征尚不清楚。

Method: 基于公开的MICA-MICs多模态MRI数据集（50名健康成人）；首先用静息态fMRI识别汇聚于丘脑、基底节及皮层下边缘结构等皮层下靶点的皮层区域；随后利用深度分辨的定量T1剖面构建汇聚受限的微结构剖面协方差矩阵，并进行扩散映射嵌入；最后结合细胞构筑分析。

Result: 第一微结构梯度主要区分初级皮层与联合皮层；第二梯度表现出超越初级-联合皮层差异的靶点相关分化；细胞构筑分析进一步揭示皮层组成的靶点相关差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了皮层-皮层下功能组织的互补维度，涵盖广泛的皮层层级、靶点相关的微结构分化以及细胞构筑组成。

Abstract: The human subcortex integrates information from distributed cortical regions through functionally organized cortico-subcortical systems. Using the publicly available MICA-MICs multimodal MRI dataset of 50 healthy adults, we characterized the microstructural organization of functionally converging cortical regions. First, using resting-state fMRI., we identified cortical regions converging on subcortical targets, including thalamic, basal ganglia, and subcortical limbic structures. Then, using depth-resolved quantitative T1 profiles, we constructed a convergence-restricted microstructural profile covariance matrix, followed by diffusion-map embedding. The first microstructural gradient predominantly differentiated primary and association cortex, whereas the second gradient showed target-related differentiation beyond differences in primary versus association cortex. Cytoarchitectural analysis further revealed target-related differences in cortical composition. Together, these findings reveal complementary dimensions of cortico-subcortical functional organization, spanning broad cortical hierarchy, target-related microstructural differentiation, and cytoarchitectural composition.

</details>


### [189] [Scale-mixture Langevin sampling in the subspaces of recurrent cortical circuit dynamics](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.28.754844v1)
*Chen, Z.,Ren, Y.,Zhang, W.-H.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该论文对连续吸引子网络（CAN）进行理论分析，发现非线性循环电路在无任何非高斯成分的条件下，内在涌现出重尾子空间动力学，可用尺度混合Langevin后验采样刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环皮层电路被认为通过随机动力学实现贝叶斯后验采样，但要判定电路内在实现的是何种采样算法，需要对生物电路动力学进行严格理论分析，而非人为将设计算法映射到电路上；由于非线性循环动力学的解析不可处理性，这仍是根本性开放难题。

Method: 选取神经科学中解析可处理的非线性循环电路动力学——连续吸引子网络（CAN）——进行综合理论分析，考察其连通性与内部变异中均无非高斯成分时的动力学行为。

Result: 电路内在涌现出重尾子空间动力学，可描述为尺度混合Langevin后验采样。机制源于电路自身非线性动力学：除法归一化使总体群体激活呈Gamma分布，并乘性地调制刺激特征子空间中Langevin采样的时间常数；该尺度混合结构产生重尾（Student-t）采样步长分布，显著加速采样。此外，在弱输入极限下，尺度混合Langevin采样趋近Lévy类机制。

Conclusion: 研究从解析上识别出嵌入非线性循环动力学子空间的内在电路算法，并确立了无需任何显式非高斯成分即可产生内源性重尾统计的最小电路机制。

Abstract: Recurrent cortical circuits are hypothesized to perform Bayesian posterior sampling through their stochastic dynamics. Yet identifying which sampling algorithm a circuit intrinsically implements, as opposed to mapping manually designing algorithms onto circuits, requires rigorous theoretical analysis of the biological circuit dynamics, and remains a fundamental open challenge because of the analytical intractability of nonlinear recurrent dynamics. Here, we tackle this challenge through comprehensive theoretical analysis of analytically tractable nonlinear recurrent circuit dynamics used in neuroscience research-- the continuous attractor network (CAN). Surprisingly, we find that the circuit, without any non-Gaussian components in its connectivity or internal variability, intrinsically emerges heavy-tailed subspaces dynamics described as scale-mixture Langevin posterior sampling. The mechanism emerges from the circuits own nonlinear dynamics: divisive normalization renders the overall population activation Gamma-distributed which multiplicatively modulates the time constant of the Langevin sampling in the stimulus feature subspace. Eventually, this scale-mixture structure produces a heavy-tailed (Student-t) sampling step size distribution, significantly accelerating the sampling speed. Moreover, the scale-mixture Langevin sampling approaches a Levy-like regime in the weak input limit. Our results analytically identify an intrinsic circuit algorithm embedded in subspaces of nonlinear recurrent dynamics, and establish a minimal circuit mechanism for endogenous heavy-tail statistics without any explicit non-Gaussian components.

</details>


### [190] [A dynamical attractor computes reward prediction in the basal forebrain](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.01.755868v1)
*Liu, H.-C.,Lin, S.-C.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 基底前脑(Npr3+)的爆发由低维动力吸引子产生：结构由环路递归动力学决定并保持不变，振幅由并发输入近似线性整合而成，实现奖励预测编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阶段性集群爆发通过群体振幅编码奖励预测，但其细胞间振幅关系（内部结构）在不同输入下保持守恒。神经系统如何在"振幅可变"与"结构守恒"这对矛盾约束间取得平衡尚不清楚。

Method: 结合光遗传学激活Npr3阳性基底前脑神经元（任务外刺激、单/双侧、与听觉线索叠加）与群体电生理记录，分析爆发的时序包络、细胞间振幅关系、起始潜伏期及振幅-刺激强度的关系。

Result: 爆发结构由递归环路动力学而非前馈输入决定；任务外光遗传激活可复现自然线索诱发爆发的低维动力学，保持时间包络与细胞间振幅关系；单侧激活非重叠Npr3群体即可招募双侧完整集群进入同一原生轨迹；同/对侧起始潜伏期均超过25 ms，提示BF之外存在递归交互；振幅随刺激强度变化并对并发单侧输入近似线性求和；该线性求和延伸至行为中自然感觉输入——叠加Npr3刺激提高线索诱发爆发振幅、改善检测并减少后续奖励反应。

Conclusion: 低维动力吸引子使基底前脑在不同输入下共享统一爆发结构，同时将并发输入整合为标量爆发振幅以编码奖励预测，为"结构守恒—振幅灵活"的矛盾提供了统一机制。

Abstract: Phasic ensemble bursting in subcortical neuromodulatory systems such as the basal forebrain (BF) provides a powerful format for broadcasting reward-prediction signals. These bursts encode reward prediction by varying population amplitude, yet their internal structure, reflected in cell-to-cell amplitude relationships, remains conserved. How neural circuits reconcile these competing constraints remains unknown. Here we show that BF bursting is generated by a dynamical attractor: burst structure is imposed not by feedforward inputs but by recurrent circuit dynamics, while burst amplitude is determined by approximately linear integration across concurrent inputs. Optogenetic activation of BF neurons expressing natriuretic peptide receptor 3 (Npr3) outside the behavioral task reproduced the low-dimensional dynamics of natural cue-evoked bursting, preserving the temporal envelope and cell-to-cell amplitude relationships across stimulation patterns. Critically, unilateral activation of non-overlapping Npr3 populations in either hemisphere recruited the full bilateral ensemble into the same native trajectory, showing that restricted inputs can nevertheless recruit the full ensemble. Matched ipsilateral and contralateral onset latencies exceeding 25 ms implicated recurrent interactions beyond the BF. Despite the conserved structure, population burst amplitude varied with stimulation strength and summed approximately linearly across concurrent unilateral inputs. This linear summation extended to natural sensory input during behavior: adding Npr3 stimulation to auditory cues increased cue-evoked burst amplitude, improved detection, and reduced subsequent reward responses, consistent with stronger reward prediction producing smaller reward prediction errors. Thus, a low-dimensional dynamical attractor preserves a shared BF burst structure across diverse inputs while integrating concurrent inputs into a scalar burst amplitude that encodes reward prediction.

</details>


### [191] [Phase-targeted Poisson closed-loop peripheral nerve stimulation modulates sensorimotor oscillations in human MEG](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.01.755613v1)
*Baker, S. N.,Zaaimi, B.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于实时MEG的闭环正中神经刺激系统，采用相位调制泊松过程，证明相位锁定的外周神经刺激能够选择性地调控感觉运动振荡，并揭示了alpha与beta振荡不同的相位-兴奋性关系及个体化峰值对齐的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环脑刺激在调控神经振荡方面具有潜力，但相位依赖性神经调控的机制尚不明确，缺乏对相位-兴奋性关系及个体化刺激参数的原理性认识。

Method: 开发基于实时MEG的闭环系统，使用相位调制泊松过程实施正中神经刺激；16名健康被试（11女5男）先进行开环表征，随后测试8个相位偏移条件（0°-315°），每个条件配对统计匹配的重放对照；并引入动态频率追踪（8-30 Hz）自动识别个体自然峰值。

Result: 闭环刺激引起稳健的相位依赖性调控（14.21±8.24%）显著高于重放对照（7.03±2.95%，p=0.0003），相位特异性在个体间一致（圆形均值70.2°）。alpha振荡（8-12 Hz）呈双向调控（0°-135°增强+21.0±19.9%，180°-315°抑制-13.3±10.8%），beta振荡（13-30 Hz）主要呈单向抑制（-9.3±9.6%）。固定频率靶向需与个体自然峰值对齐，错配会产生相位-频率耦合。动态频率追踪无需先验峰值位置即可实现稳健双向调控。

Conclusion: 实时相位锁定外周神经刺激可超越统计匹配的开环刺激，选择性调控感觉运动振荡；alpha与beta振荡的相位-兴奋性关系不同，且刺激需与个体自然峰值对齐，为个体化闭环神经调控确立了原理性指南。

Abstract: Closed-loop brain stimulation offers a promising approach for modulating neural oscillations, but the mechanisms of phase-dependent neuromodulation remain poorly understood. We developed a real-time MEG-based closed-loop system delivering median nerve stimulation using a phase-modulated Poisson process. Sixteen healthy participants (11 female, 5 male) underwent open-loop characterization followed by closed-loop testing of eight phase-offset conditions (0 degrees-315 degrees), each paired with a statistically matched replay control. Closed-loop stimulation induced robust phase-dependent modulation (14.21 +/- 8.24%) versus replay (7.03 +/- 2.95%; p = 0.0003), with consistent phase specificity across participants (circular mean: 70.2 degrees, referenced to each individual's dominant frequency). At the group level, alpha oscillations (8-12 Hz) showed bidirectional modulation (mean boost: +21.0 +/- 19.9% at 0 degrees-135 degrees; mean suppression: -13.3 +/- 10.8% at 180 degrees-315 degrees), while beta oscillations (13-30 Hz) showed predominantly unidirectional suppression (-9.3 +/- 9.6%). Successful fixed-frequency targeting required alignment with each individual's natural peak. Misalignment led to different phase shifts producing power at different frequencies, a phenomenon we term phase-frequency coupling. Dynamic frequency tracking (8-30 Hz) automatically identified and targeted each individual's natural peak, achieving robust bidirectional modulation without prior knowledge of peak location. These findings demonstrate that real-time phase-locked peripheral nerve stimulation selectively modulates sensorimotor oscillations beyond statistically matched open-loop stimulation. The distinct phase-excitability relationships of alpha and beta oscillations, together with the importance of aligning stimulation with individual natural peaks, establish principled guidelines for personalized closed-loop neuromodulation.

</details>


### [192] [The engagement of layer 6 corticothalamic neurons in somatosensation](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.11.744301v2)
*Dimwamwa, E. D.,Chang, N. H.,Waiblinger, C.,Stanley, G. B.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 在清醒小鼠体感皮层中，L6CT神经元能快速编码触觉刺激的有无并传递上行感觉信息，但对刺激细节编码较弱；其在检测任务中的反应可预测动物的行为结果，优于其他皮层兴奋性神经元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L6CT神经元是感觉皮层向丘脑的主要反馈通路之一，被假设在塑造丘脑皮层信号中起关键作用，但其在感觉加工中的具体角色仍不明确。以往研究多在麻醉状态下进行，缺乏清醒或行为状态下证据，因此需要明确L6CT在清醒动物中对感觉信息的编码特性及其与行为的关系。

Method: 在清醒小鼠上进行在体电生理记录，功能性识别体感皮层L6CT神经元，分析其对触觉刺激的反应特性（有无刺激、刺激强度/细节），并将其编码能力与其他兴奋性皮层神经元进行基准比较；同时在训练好的触觉检测任务中考察L6CT活动与行为结果的关系。

Result: L6CT神经元传递上行感觉输入的信息且时间精度足够快，可参与整个丘脑皮层环路的感觉反应；它们能稳健编码刺激的有无，但对精细细节编码较弱。与其他兴奋性皮层神经元相比，L6CT神经元对行为结果的预测能力更强，其反应强烈反映动物是否检测到刺激。

Conclusion: L6CT神经元可能不太专门负责表征精细感觉细节，而更侧重于信号化感觉事件的发生及其行为相关性，从而将L6CT与触觉检测这一通路最基本的功能角色联系起来，并为皮层丘脑反馈何时及如何参与感知提供见解。

Abstract: The corticothalamic neurons from layer 6 (L6CT) of primary sensory cortices provide extensive input to the thalamus in addition to projecting across layers intracortically, positioning them to play a key hypothesized role in shaping thalamocortical signaling. With the expansion of tools for precise functional identification of L6CT neurons during in-vivo electrophysiology, increasing evidence highlights L6CT neurons as dynamic gain modulators of thalamocortical sensory responses. However, much of the work to date has been conducted under anesthesia and not in the context of awake and/or behaving animals. In this study, we show in the awake mouse that L6CT neurons convey information about the ascending sensory input, the timing of which is fast enough to contribute to the sensory response of neurons throughout the thalamocortical circuit. Overall, L6CT neurons robustly encode the presence vs absence of a sensory stimulus but are relatively weak encoders of the fine details. Benchmarked against the activity of other excitatory cortical neurons, we also provide evidence for L6CT neurons as predictors of behavioral outcome during a trained detection task. Taken together, the results in this study potentially tie L6CT neurons to behavior in tactile detection, one of the most fundamental functional roles of the pathway.

Significance StatementLayer 6 corticothalamic (L6CT) neurons provide one of the major feedback pathways from the sensory cortex to the thalamus, yet the extent to which their role in sensory processing remains poorly understood. We show that L6CT neurons in mouse somatosensory cortex robustly signal the presence of tactile stimuli but are comparatively weak encoders of stimulus strength. During a sensory detection task, their responses strongly reflect whether the animal detects the stimulus and predict behavioral outcome better than other cortical excitatory neurons. These findings suggest that L6CT neurons are specialized less for representing fine sensory detail and more for signaling the occurrence and behavioral relevance of sensory events, providing insight into when and how corticothalamic feedback participates in perception.

</details>


### [193] [Vibrotactile transcutaneous auricular vagus nerve stimulation (taVNS) during working memory: convergent electrophysiological evidence for cortical efficiency](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.07.756069v1)
*Tan, G.,Demarest, P.,Kardashina, T.,Adams, J.,Willie, J. T.,Roland, J.,Gorlewicz, J. L.,Brunner, P.,Leuthardt, E. C.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究直接对比了taVNS改善工作记忆的两种假说：感觉放大假设与皮层效率假设。通过在健康被试与癫痫患者中同步记录EEG/iEEG与行为数据，发现主动vtavNS降低了视觉ERP与颞叶高伽马活动，且更小的视觉ERP预测了高负荷任务的编码成功，支持皮层效率假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作记忆在阿尔茨海默病、卒中及健康老化中均会衰退，taVNS是一种有前景的无创神经调控手段，被认为通过激活蓝斑-去甲肾上腺素系统等上行唤醒系统起作用。然而，taVNS调控LC-NE系统与工作记忆改善之间的生理联系仍不清楚，这一机制缺口限制了对刺激方案的理性优化以及难以确定可能受益的认知功能与患者群体。

Method: 20名健康成年人完成N-back任务，在基线、主动振动触觉taVNS（vtaVNS）和伪刺激三种平衡条件下进行，同步采集EEG、ECG、眼动与行为数据；另招募20名接受侵入性监测的癫痫患者，其中19名完成空间工作记忆任务。用头皮EEG事件相关电位（ERP）和颅内局部场电位的高伽马活动量化皮层对视觉刺激的响应，并进一步探究vtaVNS对临界性距离和心跳诱发电位的影响以检验皮层效率假说的预测。

Result: 相对于伪刺激，主动vtaVNS同时降低了头皮记录的视觉ERP与颞叶皮层的高伽马响应；更小的视觉ERP与最具挑战性工作记忆条件（4-back任务）中的成功编码相关。探索性分析显示vtaVNS使皮层动力学更接近临界状态（criticality）。

Conclusion: 结果表明vtaVNS并非均匀放大皮层对外部刺激的响应，而是提升皮层效率，支持在外感受与记忆过程之间更有效地分配认知资源。该发现为预测taVNS何时最有益以及跨认知与临床情境优化刺激方案提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Background: Working memory, the cognitive ability to maintain and manipulate a finite amount of information, is fundamental in reasoning, learning, and decision-making. Working memory erodes in conditions from Alzheimer's disease to stroke to healthy aging. Transcutaneous auricular vagus nerve stimulation (taVNS) is a promising neuromodulation method for improving working memory. By stimulating the auricular branch of the vagus nerve, taVNS is thought to engage ascending arousal systems such as the locus coeruleus-norepinephrine (LC-NE) system. However, the physiological link between LC-NE system modulation via taVNS and improved working memory remains largely unknown. This mechanistic gap limits the rational optimization of the stimulation protocol and the identification of the cognitive functions and patient populations that are likely to benefit. Objective: We test two competing hypotheses for how taVNS may improve working memory: sensory amplification or enhancement of cortical efficiency. The sensory-amplification hypothesis predicts increased cortical responses to stimuli, whereas the cortical-efficiency hypothesis predicts reduced cortical responses. Methods: Twenty healthy adults completed N-back working memory tasks during three counterbalanced sessions: baseline, active vibrotactile taVNS (vtaVNS), and sham stimulation, while we simultaneously recorded electroencephalography, electrocardiography, eye-tracking, and behavioral responses. To study the effect of vtaVNS on neuronal activity, we also recruited 20 patients with epilepsy undergoing invasive monitoring. Of these, 19 fully completed the spatial working-memory task. We quantified cortical responses to visual stimuli using event-related potentials (ERPs) in scalp EEG and high-gamma activity in intracranial local field potentials. To examine prediction of the cortical-efficiency hypothesis, we further explored the effect of vtaVNS on distance to criticality and heartbeat-evoked potentials. Results: Active vtaVNS reduced both scalp-recorded visual ERP and high-gamma response in temporal cortices, relative to sham stimulation. Moreover, smaller visual event-related potentials were associated with successful encoding during the most demanding working-memory condition (i.e., 4-back task). Exploratory analyses showed that vtaVNS shifted cortical dynamics closer to criticality. Conclusion: Our results converge on the conclusion that vtaVNS does not uniformly amplify cortical responses to external stimuli. Instead, vtaVNS enhances cortical efficiency, supporting more effective allocation of cognitive resources between exteroceptive and mnemonic processes. These findings provide a framework for predicting when taVNS will be most beneficial and for optimizing stimulation protocols across cognitive and clinical applications.

</details>


### [194] [Streamlined Production of Recombinant Adeno-Associated Viruses Using Gateway Cloning Technology for Neural Circuit All-Optical Interrogation](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.22.752944v2)
*Ayon, A.,Jalil, A.,Egan, C.,Ducos, B.,Mailhes-Hamon, C.,Tangara, A.,Mathieu, B.,Bemelmans, A.-P.,Llano, I.,Bourdieu, L.,Dieudonne, S.,Villette, V.,Bradley, J.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于Gateway的重组AAV载体构建平台，通过启动子、指示器和衣壳的模块化组合实现神经元群体高效表达，并结合ULoVE双光子显微技术，在小脑回路中实现亚毫秒级电压/钙成像、单细胞光遗传激活及轴突投射解剖示踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光遗传学与基因编码指示器推动了神经科学研究，但其成功应用依赖于在特定神经元群中高效表达报告基因。现有方法在启动子选择、指示器工程和衣壳优化上缺乏系统、快速的构建管线，难以满足如ULoVE等对细胞类型特异性、稀疏标记和表达水平精确控制有严苛要求的光学工具。因此需要一套模块化、可组合的AAV表达平台。

Method: 开发基于Gateway(R)的克隆平台，用于快速高效构建面向神经科学的rAAV载体，支持启动子选择、指示器工程与衣壳类型优化，并可对不同光学工具组件进行系统性优化。将该平台与基于声光偏转器（AOD）的ULoVE双光子显微方法结合：用Gateway构建的Cre驱动病毒搭配Cre依赖报告基因，实现细胞类型特异性标记。在小脑浦肯野细胞中，L7::Cre驱动病毒配合电压指示器JEDI2P-Kv与约5 kHz ULoVE双光子激发；在急性小脑切片中，kit::Cre驱动病毒驱动ChR2(H134R)在分子层中间神经元表达，并采用ULoVE环形（doughnut）光刺激。

Result: 在清醒行为小鼠中，L7::Cre+JEDI2P-Kv+ULoVE实现亚毫秒级树突电压动态解析，光学检测到此前仅能通过胞内电生理获得的树突spikelet；同一驱动病毒配合钙指示器GCaMP6f、jRGECO1a可解析攀爬纤维诱发的钙动力学。在急性小脑切片中，kit::Cre驱动ChR2(H134R)实现单细胞、亚毫秒级光遗传激活，空间分辨率达微米级。此外，该稀疏强标记策略还适用于透明化小脑组织的轴突投射解剖示踪，可在颗粒细胞层-分子层边界分辨单个DCN轴突末梢。

Conclusion: 该研究提出“模块化AAV表达管线+AOD光学采集”的双前沿策略，构成一个集成平台，可用于开发包括但不限于AOD显微在内的复杂光学工具，服务于神经回路解析。

Abstract: The advent of optogenetics and the rapid development of genetically encoded actuators and reporters for calcium, voltage, neurotransmitters, and other molecules have revolutionized neuroscience research by enabling precise, non-invasive optical interrogation of neural circuits. Successful implementation, however, critically depends on efficient reporter expression in defined neuronal populations. We present a Gateway(R)-based cloning platform providing a robust pipeline for rapid, efficient construction of recombinant adeno-associated virus (rAAV) vectors optimized for neuroscience applications. This platform addresses promoter selection, indicator engineering, and capsid-type optimization, and enables systematic optimization of vector components across a range of optical tools. The benefit of this modular approach is illustrated in the case of ULoVE, a two-photon microscopy method based on acousto-optic deflectors (AODs). This method provides serial light-targeting with kHz sampling rates and high signal-to-noise ratio in vivo, which imposes stringent requirements on indicator expression -- including cell-type specificity, sparse labelling, and precise control of expression levels-- efficiently met through the combinatorial flexibility of the Gateway pipeline. As specific examples, Gateway-constructed Cre-driver viruses combined with Cre-dependent reporters enabled cell-type-specific labelling for two complementary applications. In cerebellar Purkinje cells, an L7::Cre driver virus paired with a Gateway-constructed voltage indicator (JEDI2P-Kv) and ULoVE two-photon excitation at ~5 kHz enables resolution of sub-millisecond dendritic voltage dynamics in awake, behaving mice, including optical detection of dendritic spikelets previously accessible only via intracellular electrophysiology. The same driver virus paired with calcium indicators (GCaMP6f, jRGECO1a) resolves climbing fiber-evoked calcium kinetics under the same conditions. In acute cerebellar slices, a kit::Cre driver virus enabled cell-type-specific expression of the optogenetic actuator ChR2(H134R) in molecular layer interneurons, where ULoVE doughnut-pattern photostimulation achieved single-cell, sub-millisecond optogenetic activation with micron-scale spatial resolution.

Beyond ULoVE applications, the same sparse, strong labelling also proved suitable for anatomical tracing of axonal projections in cleared cerebellar tissue, resolving individual DCN axon terminals at the granule cell layer-molecular layer boundary. Taken together, our dual-front approach--combining a modular AAV expression pipeline with AOD-based optical acquisition--provides an integrated platform for developing sophisticated optical tools, including but not limited to AOD-based microscopy, for neural circuit interrogation.

</details>


### [195] [Selective Hippocampal Reactivation Driven by Neocortical Feedback Supports Memory Transfer in Predictive-Coding Networks](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.29.755342v2)
*Paul, S.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 将海马与新皮层建模为耦合的预测编码联想记忆网络，通过非对称耦合形成闭环对话，证明新皮层反馈可将海马活动导向高自由能状态，从而自发实现记忆优先重现并支持高效的系统巩固。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠与静息觉醒时大脑会自发重现与过往经验相关的神经活动模式（reactivation/replay），该过程被认为支持系统巩固，使记忆提取逐渐依赖新皮层而非海马。重现具有选择性，优先针对新的或学习较弱的记忆。既有计算模型分别解释了记忆的重现与优先化，以及预测编码框架下海马-新皮层计算的涌现，但两条线索尚未连通，本文旨在将二者结合。

Method: 将海马与新皮层分别建模为两个耦合的预测编码（PC）联想记忆网络，耦合为非对称形式，依据海马CA1与前额叶皮层之间已报道的相互作用。该耦合构成闭环对话：海马活动驱动新皮层学习，新皮层反馈则偏置后续海马活动，使其指向新皮层支持仍较弱的内容。通过理论分析证明该反馈将海马活动导向新皮层自由能更高的状态。

Result: 新皮层学习完全依赖自发的海马活动，不接触原始训练语料。结果显示，新皮层网络获取相关记忆内容的速度快于对随机采样记忆的直接训练；该优势在记忆相关性足够高时出现，并随相关性增强而增大，且在迁移过程中逐步加入新记忆时依然保持。优先化由动力学自然涌现，而非依赖显式调度器。

Conclusion: 研究提供了预测编码框架下的解释：新皮层反馈组织海马重现，从而在系统巩固期间支持高效的记忆迁移。

Abstract: During sleep and quiet wakefulness, the brain spontaneously revisits neural activity patterns associated with previous experience, a phenomenon known as reactivation, or replay. Reactivation is thought to support systems consolidation, the process by which memory retrieval becomes progressively more dependent on neocortical circuits and less dependent on the hippocampus. Importantly, reactivation is not indiscriminate: it preferentially targets specific memories, such as new or weakly learned ones.

These observations have motivated the development of computational models accounting for the spontaneous reactivation of memories, their prioritisation for replay, and their transfer between the hippocampus and neocortex. In parallel, predictive-coding (PC) models have shown how computations attributed to hippocampal and neocortical circuits can arise from local prediction-error dynamics. Here, we connect these two lines of work by showing how autonomous intrinsic dynamics in coupled PC networks can transfer stored memory content between neural systems.

The hippocampus and neocortex are modelled as two coupled PC associative memory networks, with asymmetric coupling motivated by reported interactions between hippocampal area CA1 and the prefrontal cortex. This coupling establishes a closed-loop dialogue in which hippocampal activity drives neocortical learning, while neocortical feedback biases subsequent hippocampal activity toward content for which neocortical support remains weak. We prove that this feedback steers hippocampal activity toward states of higher neocortical free energy, so that prioritisation emerges from the dynamics rather than from an explicit scheduler.

In our model, neocortical learning relies exclusively on spontaneous hippocampal activity, without access to the initial training corpus. Nevertheless, we show that the neocortical network acquires correlated memory content faster than direct training on randomly sampled memories. This advantage appeared for sufficiently correlated memories, grew with their correlation, and persisted when new memories were incrementally added during transfer.

Together, these results provide a PC account in which neocortical feedback organises hippocampal reactivation to support efficient memory transfer during systems consolidation.

</details>


### [196] [Thalamocortical credit routing from the basal ganglia and cerebellum: anatomical constraints and circuit principles](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.02.755501v1)
*Tanno, S.,Morishima, M.,Fujita, K.,Kato, S.,Kobayashi, K.,Kawaguchi, Y.,Isomura, Y.,Hira, R.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 研究结合小鼠顺行跨突触示踪与闭环回路建模，揭示丘脑皮层通路如何将皮层下学习信号路由回特定皮层回路以实现信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物在复杂环境中需选择能最大化奖励的动作，核心难题是"信用分配"：行为结果必须回传到产生相关动作的神经群体。基底神经节和小脑中的可塑性由奖励预测误差塑造，可能通过丘脑皮层通路传递学习信号，但这些通路如何将信号回传到特定皮层回路尚不清楚。

Method: 结合小鼠解剖回路映射与闭环回路建模。用顺行跨突触示踪追踪接受黑质网状部或外侧小脑核输入的丘脑神经元及其皮层轴突分布；并构建全脑协同回路（loop-circuit）模型，检验不同反馈方式对学习的影响，包括局部活动校准、树突棘波与奖励预测误差依赖的丘脑皮层可塑性。

Result: 接受黑质网状部与外侧小脑核输入的丘脑神经元大体分离，仅在腹内侧丘脑部分重叠；其皮层轴突共同显著富集于第1a层，尤以次级运动皮层最明显。回路模型中，当两条通路以加性和增益调制反馈并与皮层动作读出对齐时学习增强；局部活动校准建立该对齐，树突棘波与奖励预测误差依赖的丘脑皮层可塑性进一步优化行为输出。模块特异性反馈对需同时条件化多个目标神经元的脑机接口任务必不可少。

Conclusion: 研究界定了一种信用分配架构：层状汇聚、可塑性对齐与模块化反馈将并行皮层下学习系统连接至皮层动作表征。

Abstract: Animals learn to select actions that maximize reward in complex environments. A central challenge in this process is credit routing: behavioral outcomes must be routed back to the neural populations that contributed to generating the relevant actions. One possible route is through thalamocortical pathways conveying learning signals from the basal ganglia and cerebellum, where plasticity is shaped by reward-prediction errors. However, how these pathways route such signals back to specific cortical circuits remains unclear. To address this question, we combined anatomical circuit mapping in mice with closed-loop circuit modeling. Anterograde transsynaptic tracing revealed that thalamic neurons receiving input from the substantia nigra pars reticulata or the lateral cerebellar nucleus were largely segregated, with partial overlap in the ventromedial thalamus. Nevertheless, their cortical axons shared a pronounced enrichment in layer 1a, most prominently in secondary motor cortex. In a brain- wide synergistic loop-circuit model, learning was enhanced when both pathways delivered additive and gain-modulating feedback aligned with the cortical action readout. Local activity- based calibration established this alignment, whereas dendritic-spike- and reward-prediction- error-dependent thalamocortical plasticity further refined behavioral output. Module-specific feedback was essential for a brain-machine interface task requiring the simultaneous conditioning of multiple target neurons. Together, these findings define a credit-routing architecture in which laminar convergence, plastic alignment, and modular feedback link parallel subcortical learning systems to cortical action representations.

</details>


### [197] [Flexible 3D multimodal organoid interface reveals functional subnetworks and coordinated circuit activity in cortical organoids](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.02.756317v1)
*Wilson, M. N.,Kharitonova, E. K.,Yeh, J.,Lee, J.,Hemaidan, S.,Essig, S.,Shi, M.,Crain, C. W.,Ramezani, M.,Sun, F.,Zhou, T.,Surucu, I.,Mortazavi, F.,Cubukcu, E.,Thunemann, M.,Zeldich, E.,Kuzum, D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该论文报道了INFORM——一种可共形包裹任意尺寸与形状类器官的多模态神经接口平台，实现了对脑类器官中兴奋/抑制（E/I）神经网络的长期、多尺度、单细胞级功能解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E/I神经元平衡是皮层功能的基础，其失调与癫痫、阿尔茨海默病、自闭症谱系障碍等多种神经系统疾病相关。皮层类器官可重现E/I环路形成等脑发育关键特征，是理想模型；但现有技术无法在完整类器官内同时解析神经元身份、空间组织与动态网络活动，制约了对E/I环路功能的精确探究。

Method: 提出INFORM（INtegrated Flexible Organoid Recording Multimodal）柔性集成类器官记录多模态神经接口，可共形包裹任意尺寸和形状的类器官，支持长期记录（超过371天，>90%通道可检测到spike）。该接口整合四种模态：电生理记录、双光子钙成像、靶向电微刺激以及化学干预，覆盖从单神经元动作电位到大规模群体同步爆发的多时空尺度，可对神经活动进行监测与调控。

Result: INFORM实现了高分辨率功能解析：可探测融合类组装体（assembloids）中功能连接的涌现，并分辨出可被选择性募集和调控的空间离散功能子网络；通过神经递质阻断实验证实，刺激诱发的神经元放电抑制由GABA能输入介导，为类器官中存在抑制性信号提供了直接功能证据。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了脑类器官功能网络的单细胞级解析，为建模和研究皮层发育、神经系统疾病以及环路功能障碍背后的E/I失衡提供了强有力的平台。

Abstract: The balance between excitatory and inhibitory (E/I) neurons is fundamental to cortical function, and its disruption underlies a wide range of neurological disorders, including epilepsy, Alzheimers disease and autism spectrum disorder. Cortical organoids offer a promising model to study this balance, as they recapitulate key features of brain development, including the emergence of E/I circuitry. However, no existing technology can simultaneously resolve neuronal identity, spatial organization, and dynamic network activity within intact organoids, limiting precise interrogation of E/I circuit function. Here, we report INFORM, an INtegrated Flexible Organoid Recording Multimodal neural interface that conformally wraps organoids of any size and shape, enabling long-term recordings over 371 days with above 90% of channels detecting spikes. INFORM interface combines four distinct modalities; electrophysiology, two-photon calcium imaging, targeted electrical microstimulation, and chemical intervention to monitor and modulate neural activity across multiple spatial and temporal scales spanning from action potentials from single neurons to synchronized bursts from large-scale populations. Such high-resolution interrogation enables probing of emergence of functional connectivity in fusing assembloids and resolves spatially discrete functional subnetworks that can be selectively recruited and modulated by INFORM. Neurotransmission blockade confirms that stimulus-evoked suppression of neuronal spiking is mediated by GABAergic inputs providing direct functional evidence of the presence of inhibitory signaling in organoids. This work allows single-cell dissection of functional networks in brain organoids, providing a powerful platform for modelling and studying cortical development, neurological diseases, and the E/I imbalances underlying circuit dysfunction.

</details>


### [198] [Selective vulnerability of intracortical projection neurons drives long-term cortical circuit rewiring after perinatal hypoxia](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.21.753115v2)
*Ellouze, S.,Babina, E.,Meyer-Dilhet, G.,Foucault, L.,Amara, A.,Desmerciere, S.,Gagnieu, T.,Mongellaz, S.,Cataldi, A.,Marcy, G.,Ghersi-Egea, J. F.,Courchet, J.,Raineteau, O.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究利用慢性围产期缺氧小鼠模型发现，早发生命中的脑损伤虽在皮层厚度上可短期恢复，但在转录、皮层连接及行为层面留下持久性改变，揭示了早发损伤如何通过改变皮层发育导致远期行为缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中枢神经系统在围产期关键可塑性阶段的损伤通常比成年期损伤恢复更好，但常伴随长期行为缺陷，提示皮层神经环路的重新连接可能不完整或不恰当。作者希望验证这一可能性，探究早发脑损伤对皮层发育的长期影响及其与行为缺陷的关系。

Method: 采用慢性围产期缺氧小鼠模型（模拟极早产），检测皮层厚度变化；在缺氧后短期（P11）和长期（P45）时间点进行皮层单核转录组分析；结合顺行与逆行示踪及线粒体标记的组织学分析，评估皮层连接；并进行行为学测试。

Result: 慢性缺氧导致皮层厚度下降（类似极早产儿），但在8天后快速恢复。转录组分析显示神经元中存在持久性转录改变，涉及线粒体代谢、轴突生成和突触生成相关基因。组织学分析表明上层皮层神经元存在持久性改变，导致成年缺氧小鼠皮层内皮质-皮质连接增加。行为测试显示缺氧小鼠社交行为改变，且随年龄加重。

Conclusion: 早发生命中的脑损伤会改变正常皮层发育，并对皮层连接产生长期影响，进而促成在生命后期出现的行为缺陷，揭示了围产期损伤导致远期功能障碍的机制。

Abstract: Cortical circuits are built at perinatal times and gradually refined in an activity-dependent manner during a so-called postnatal period of critical plasticity. Although lesions of the central nervous system (CNS) happening during this period typically recover better than those occurring later in life, they are often associated with long-term behavioral deficits. This suggests that neuronal circuits rewiring, in particular within the cortex, may either be incomplete or inappropriate.

To address this possibility, we used chronic perinatal hypoxia, a mouse model of very premature birth. We confirmed that chronic hypoxia induced a decrease in cortical thickness frequently observed in very preterm babies, which rapidly recovered 8 days later. To explore the transcriptional correlates of this recovery we next performed singlenuclei transcriptomic analysis of the cortex at short (P11) and long (P45) timepoints following hypoxia. This revealed persistent transcriptional changes within neurons, including of genes involved in mitochondrial metabolism, axonogenesis and synaptogenesis. Further, histological analysis using anterograde and retrograde tracing as well as mitochondrial labelling support persistent alterations in upper cortical neurons resulting in increased corticocortical connectivity within the cortex of adult hypoxic mice. Finally, behavioral testing revealed altered social behavior in mice exposed to chronic hypoxia, which amplified with age.

Altogether, our results unravel how brain lesions happening early in life, alter normal cortical development and have long term consequences on cortical wiring, contributing to the observed behaviors defects appearing later in life.

</details>

### [199] [Interpretable Memory Models for Spaced Repetition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10548)
*Anders Schill*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出 SBD 记忆模型，用于间隔重复复习排程；在准确率接近当前最优模型的前提下，参数量减少 80% 且机制解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间隔重复软件依赖拟合复习日志的记忆模型来安排复习。现有评估仅看测试集准确率并不充分：数据本身由既有排程器产生，存在分布偏差，新方案必须外推到训练分布之外才能证明其价值；同时模型还需要具备简洁的机制解释。

Method: 提出 SBD 模型，在保持记忆预测准确率的同时，通过更简洁的建模范式实现可解释的机制解释，并将模型规模较当前最优缩小 80%。

Result: SBD 的准确率与当前最优模型几乎持平，但模型体积小 80%，且可解释性更强。

Conclusion: 在间隔重复排程场景中，单纯追求测试集准确率不足以衡量模型质量；SBD 表明可在几乎不损失精度的条件下获得更小、更可解释的记忆模型。

Abstract: Spaced repetition software schedules reviews with a memory model fit to review logs. Accuracy on a test set is not sufficient evidence of quality since available data are produced by existing schedulers, and new solutions must extrapolate beyond them. A model also needs a simple mechanistic interpretation. We present SBD, a model that is more interpretable and 80% smaller than the current state of the art at nearly the same accuracy.

</details>


### [200] [Mean Field Theory Based on the Spike Time Response Curve for Synchronization Within and Between Two Alternating Populations of Neural Oscillators with Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10977)
*Ananth Vedururu Srinivas,Carmen C. Canavier*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文将用于单同步种群的神经振荡器平均场方法扩展到两个交替同步种群，用以研究双指数突触在高频下脉冲效应跨周期求和时的锁相问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脉冲耦合振荡器模型假设每个脉冲对相位产生瞬时偏移，仅考虑发射与接收间的传导延迟。但真实神经振荡器中的脉冲激活双指数突触，在高频下突触电导持续时间可能超过网络周期，导致接收脉冲效应在多个周期上求和，瞬时相移假设失效。

Method: 将单个同步种群的平均场方法扩展到两个交替同步种群：以单个自连接神经振荡器表示其中一个同步种群中单个振荡器的扰动；将每个振荡器发射的延迟双指数突触序列分解为紧张性和相位性两个分量；由于输入是连续而非脉冲式的，放弃振荡器相位概念，仅基于时间间隔相对稳态值的扰动，推导两个同步种群间交替放电模式存在性与稳定性的自洽判据。

Result: 仿真表明，在上述定义的高频振荡条件下，平均场方法比瞬时相移方法能更好地预测两个同步种群之间锁相的存在性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 平均场方法可有效处理突触电导持续时间超过网络周期时的脉冲效应跨周期求和问题，并可能推广到非神经振荡器之间的其他耦合形式。

Abstract: In order to study oscillators coupled by pulses emitted at discrete times, it is generally assumed that the effect of each pulse is an instantaneous phase shift, with a possible conduction delay between emission and receipt. However, we are interested in neural oscillators in which each pulse (action potential) activates a biexponential synapse, and at high frequencies, the duration of the synaptic conductance may be greater than the network period. Therefore, the effect of received pulses can summate over the course of several cycles. To overcome this limitation, we previously defined a mean field approach for a single synchronous population. Here, we extend it to two alternating synchronous populations by assuming a perturbation of a single oscillator from one of the synchronous populations which is represented by a single self-connected neural oscillator. The train of delayed biexponential synapses emitted by each oscillator is divided into a tonic and a phasic component. Because of the continuous rather than pulsatile nature of the input, we had to drop the concept of oscillator phase and derive self-consistent criteria for the existence and stability of an alternating firing pattern between two synchronous populations based solely on the perturbation of time intervals from their steady state values. Simulations showed that for high frequency oscillations as defined above, the mean field approach predicts the existence and stability of phase-locking between two synchronous populations better than the instantaneous phase shift approach. This approach may generalize to other forms of coupling amongst non-neural oscillators.

</details>


### [201] [If the brain were so simple: Information-based drivers of intelligence and their interaction through an n-body-inspired framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11142)
*Brett J. Kagan,Johnson Zhou,Forough Habibollahi,Valentina Baccetti*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文从信息学与算法热力学视角出发，提出"信息焓"及"信息焓势(IEP)"概念，用于刻画神经系统如何响应不同信息模式，并给出可量化方法与可证伪的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于大脑信息处理与智能输出的理论众多，但往往难以被决定性地检验。作者希望构建一个以信息学和算法热力学为基础、且具备可证伪性的框架，来解释神经系统为何对不同信息模式产生不同响应，并弥补既有理论在检验性上的不足。

Method: 引入类比热力学的核心概念：将信号的信息熵与"信息焓"（系统内部可用于预测及其他形式工作的结构化信息资源）相联系；提出"信息焓势(IEP)"作为衡量外部信号提升系统信息焓能力的度量，并在多种示例信号上实现该指标的量化方法。随后，作者给出神经系统在生物学框架下处理信息焓的猜想，并将其与自由能原理等既有方法进行整合与可证伪性讨论，同时结合神经临界性视角。

Result: 提出并实现了IEP的量化方法，在多种示例信号上展示了该势能的测量；论证了信息焓框架可为自由能原理等现有理论提供整合与可证伪性；并提出一个受n体模型启发、由多个驱动因子相互作用、支配神经系统在高维状态空间中"运动"的可检验、可证伪框架。

Conclusion: 作者认为，上述信息焓、IEP及相关驱动机制构成了神经驱动的最基本基础，这些驱动共同产生了被称为"智能"的复杂适应性行为，从而为理解智能提供了一个统一且可证伪的理论起点。

Abstract: Theories of how the brain processes information and returns intelligent outputs are numerous and often difficult to conclusively test. Here, we consider an approach grounded in informatics and algorithmic thermodynamics for how neural systems respond to different patterns of information in different ways, with a focus on the relationship between the information entropy of a signal and a new proposed quantity we term information enthalpy, which represents the internal resource of structured information within a system available to be used for predictions and other forms of work. To evaluate the capacity of the external signals to increase information enthalpy in the system, we introduce the information enthalpy potential (IEP) as a defined metric. We propose and implement a method for quantifying the amount of potential information enthalpy - the IEP - in a given signal across a range of example signals. We offer a conjecture of how information enthalpy may be treated by neural systems within a biological framework before showing how it may integrate and offer falsifiability to existing approaches such as the Free Energy Principle. This framework is also positioned in light of the role of neural criticality. Finally, we postulate a testable and falsifiable framework where the distinct mechanisms within each driver interact through a described n-body-inspired model to govern the "motion" of a neural system through a high dimensional state-space. Through these processes, it is proposed that these features form the most fundamental basis of the neural drivers that give rise to the complexity of adaptive behaviors that are commonly called: intelligence.

</details>


### [202] [Neural Decoding as Cognitive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11923)
*Yi Guo,Changhong Jing,Yong Hu,Yan Liu,Michael K. P. Ng,Shanshan Wang,Shuqiang Wang*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究提出将神经解码重构为受脑内先验约束的认知推断，从而获得跨模态、跨认知域稳定的元神经语义表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑能在神经活动持续变化的情况下保持稳定认知，但如何从可变神经观测中提取稳定认知状态仍是神经解码的核心难题。现有方法基于刺激-反应原则将神经观测映射到预设外部标签，往往学到与特定记录相关的伪相关。

Method: 受贝叶斯大脑理论启发，将神经解码重新表述为受脑内先验约束的认知推断，进而产生高层次的元神经语义表征。

Result: 在涵盖五种神经记录模态、三个认知域（运动、知觉与内部心理活动）的解码实验中，该方法重塑了神经观测表征的几何结构，得到的元神经语义表征在不同认知任务间呈现一致的几何关系，并能从可变的神经观测中恢复稳定的认知状态。

Conclusion: 该工作解释了大脑如何在外部环境不断变化时维持相对稳定的认知：认知稳定性通过从变化神经活动中进行认知推断而得以维持，无需固定的神经活动模式。

Abstract: The brain maintains stable cognition despite continuously changing neural activity. How to extract stable cognitive states from variable neural observations remains a central problem in neural decoding. Existing neural decoding methods map neural observations to predefined external labels based on the stimulus-response principle, often capturing recording-specific spurious correlations. Inspired by how the brain infers the world, and specifically by Bayesian brain theory, we recast neural decoding as cognitive inference constrained by brain-intrinsic priors, yielding high-level meta-neural semantic representations. In decoding experiments spanning five neural recording modalities and three cognitive domains (motor, perception and internal mentation), our cognitive inference method reorganized the geometry of neural observation representations, yielding meta-neural semantic representations that exhibited consistent geometric relationships across cognitive tasks and enabled the recovery of stable cognitive states from variable neural observations. Our work provides an account of how the brain maintains relatively stable cognition despite continual changes in the external environment. Cognitive stability is sustained through cognitive inference from changing neural activity, without requiring fixed neural activity patterns.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Learning to Orchestrate Evolutionary Search: Progression-Aware Deep Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic DE-CMA-ES Coordination in Optimization and Structural Model Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11546)
*Lechen Li,Rongye Shi,Wanhuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出DRL-DCO算法，用深度强化学习动态调度DE与CMA-ES，统一编排进化过程，在高维优化与结构健康监测基准上取得更优收敛精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维结构模型更新问题具有复杂非凸、参数相关的搜索空间，而现有混合进化算法多依赖静态架构或固定切换规则，导致搜索阶段割裂、探索与开发难以协调。

Method: 构建由DDPG驱动的actor-critic智能体，将进化过程视为统一系统而非算法机械拼接；采用进展感知状态表示与多样性驱动奖励，动态在DE的差分向量探索与CMA-ES的协方差引导开发间重分配计算资源，同时调控种群规模、精英保留与重启机制；训练后可进入无监督推理模式，仅靠前向推断编排控制。

Result: 在高维单目标优化基准与IASC-ASCE结构健康监测基准上，DRL-DCO的收敛精度与鲁棒性优于先进自适应算法、混合进化算法及单算子DRL基线。

Conclusion: DRL-DCO通过强化学习统一编排DE与CMA-ES，实现探索主导、开发主导与混合策略的自主动态切换，并可无监督部署，为高维结构模型更新提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Solving high-dimensional structural model updating problems requires an algorithm capable of navigating complex, non-convex landscapes with correlated parameters. Existing hybrid evolutionary algorithms typically rely on static architectures or fixed switching rules, resulting in disjointed search phases. To address this, this study proposes a Deep Reinforcement Learning-governed dynamic DE-CMAES Orchestration (DRL-DCO) algorithm, in which a Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG)-based actor-critic agent continuously governs the evolutionary process as a single, unified system rather than a mechanical concatenation of algorithms. Guided by a progression-aware state representation and a diversity-informed reward, the agent fluidly reallocates computational resources between the differencevector-based exploration of Differential Evolution (DE) and the covariance-guided exploitation of CMA-ES, while jointly regulating population size, elite preservation, and a restart mechanism to escape local optima. This allows DRL-DCO to autonomously transition between exploration-dominant, exploitation-dominant, and mixed-strategy regimes across generations. Beyond the training phase, the trained actor can operate in a supervision-free inference mode, where the internalized policy autonomously orchestrates DE and CMA-ES control from observed search states through forward inference alone, without critic evaluation or weight updates, enabling faster deployment while retaining full effectiveness. Validated on high-dimensional single-objective optimization benchmarks and the IASC-ASCE structural health monitoring benchmark, DRL-DCO achieves superior convergence accuracy and robustness compared to state-of-the-art adaptive and hybrid evolutionary algorithms, as well as single-operator DRL-governed baselines.

</details>
